            fade_out_secs: 0.0,
        });
        let after_nested = audio_cue(ContinueMode::Nothing);
        let outer = Cue::new_typed(CueType::Group {
            mode: GroupMode::Normal,
            children: vec![nested.id, after_nested.id],
            fade_in_secs: 0.0,
            fade_out_secs: 0.0,
        });
        let boundary_id = nested_children[1].id;
        let mut cues = nested_children;
        cues.push(nested.clone());
        cues.push(after_nested);
        cues.push(outer.clone());

        assert_eq!(
            normal_group_manual_stop_in(&cues, outer.id, &mut HashSet::new()),
            Some((vec![outer.id, nested.id], boundary_id))
        );
    }
}


// === FILE: D:\winlab\cuelabs\src\main.rs ===

// CueLabs вЂ” Professional Audio Cue Player
// Entry point

#![cfg_attr(not(debug_assertions), windows_subsystem = "windows")]

use eframe::egui;
use tracing_subscriber::EnvFilter;

mod app;
mod audio;
mod config;
mod cue;
mod export;
mod midi;
mod osc;
mod project;
mod ui;

fn main() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    // Logging
    tracing_subscriber::fmt()
        .with_env_filter(EnvFilter::new("cuelabs=debug,warn"))
        .init();

    let options = eframe::NativeOptions {
        viewport: egui::ViewportBuilder::default()
            .with_title("CueLabs")
            .with_inner_size([1400.0, 900.0])
            .with_min_inner_size([900.0, 600.0])
            .with_drag_and_drop(true) // в†ђ РїРµСЂРµРЅРµСЃР»Рё СЃСЋРґР°
            .with_icon(eframe::icon_data::from_png_bytes(&[]).unwrap_or_default()),
        ..Default::default()
    };

    eframe::run_native(
        "CueLabs",
        options,
        Box::new(|cc| Ok(Box::new(app::CueLabsApp::new(cc)))),
    )
    .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("eframe error: {e}"))
}

// src/audio/engine.rs вЂ” Р°СѓРґРёРѕРґРІРёР¶РѕРє СЃ РїРѕРґРґРµСЂР¶РєРѕР№ Fade In/Out, Start/End, Loop Рё СЃРјРµРЅС‹ I/O РєР°СЂС‚

use crate::config::AudioBackend;
use crate::cue::{ChannelRoute, CueState, FadeCurve, OutputChannelSetting};
use anyhow::Result;
use cpal::traits::{DeviceTrait, HostTrait};
use cpal::{Device, Host};
use crossbeam_channel::{bounded, Receiver, Sender};
use parking_lot::RwLock;
use rodio::Source;

use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum AudioCommand {
    Play {
        id: Uuid,
        file: PathBuf,
        /// None uses the global output device; Some selects a per-cue device.
        output_device: Option<String>,
        start_secs: f64,
        end_secs: Option<f64>,
        duration_secs: f64,
        volume: f32,
        fade_in_secs: f64,
        fade_out_secs: f64,
        fade_curve: FadeCurve,
        custom_fade_points: Vec<[f32; 2]>,
        channel_routes: Vec<ChannelRoute>,
        loop_enabled: bool,
        loop_start: f64,
        loop_end: f64,
        loop_count: i32, // -1 = Р±РµСЃРєРѕРЅРµС‡РЅС‹Р№, >0 = С‡РёСЃР»Рѕ РїРѕРІС‚РѕСЂРѕРІ
    },
    Stop(Uuid),
    StopAll,
    Pause(Uuid),
    Seek {
        id: Uuid,
        position_secs: f64,
    },
    PauseAll,
    Resume(Uuid),
    ResumeAll,
    SetVolume(Uuid, f32),
    Fade {
        id: Uuid,
        target_volume: f32,
        duration_secs: f64,
        stop_on_finish: bool,
        curve: FadeCurve,
        custom_fade_points: Vec<[f32; 2]>,
    },
    SetOutputDevice(String),
    SetInputDevice(String),
    SetRouting {
        id: Uuid,
        routes: Vec<ChannelRoute>,
    },
    Shutdown,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct PositionUpdate {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub position_secs: f64,
    pub state: CueState,
}

fn host_for_backend(backend: AudioBackend) -> Result<Host> {
    #[cfg(all(target_os = "windows", feature = "asio"))]
    {
        let host_id = match backend {
            AudioBackend::Wasapi => cpal::HostId::Wasapi,
            AudioBackend::Asio => cpal::HostId::Asio,
        };
        return cpal::host_from_id(host_id).map_err(|error| {
            anyhow::anyhow!(
                "Could not initialise {} audio host: {}",
                backend.label(),
                error
            )
        });
    }
    #[cfg(all(target_os = "windows", not(feature = "asio")))]
    {
        match backend {
            AudioBackend::Wasapi => Ok(cpal::host_from_id(cpal::HostId::Wasapi)
                .map_err(|error| anyhow::anyhow!("Could not initialise WASAPI host: {}", error))?),
            AudioBackend::Asio => Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
                "ASIO support is not compiled in. Rebuild CueLabs with `--features asio`."
            )),
        }
    }
    #[cfg(not(target_os = "windows"))]
    {
        match backend {
            AudioBackend::Wasapi => Ok(cpal::default_host()),
            AudioBackend::Asio => Err(anyhow::anyhow!("ASIO backend is only available on Windows")),
        }
    }
}

fn supported_output_channel_layouts(device: &Device) -> Vec<usize> {
    let mut layouts: Vec<usize> = device
        .supported_output_configs()
        .map(|configs| configs.map(|config| config.channels() as usize).collect())
        .unwrap_or_default();
    if let Ok(default_config) = device.default_output_config() {
        layouts.push(default_config.channels() as usize);
    }
    layouts.retain(|channels| *channels > 0);
    layouts.sort_unstable();
    layouts.dedup();
    layouts
}

fn preferred_output_config(device: &Device) -> Option<cpal::SupportedStreamConfig> {
    // Select the configuration with the greatest number of channels first;
    // prefer F32 only when two configurations expose the same channel count.
    let config = device
        .supported_output_configs()
        .ok()?
        .max_by_key(|config| {
            (
                config.channels(),
                (config.sample_format() == cpal::SampleFormat::F32) as u8,
            )
        })?;
    let min_rate = config.min_sample_rate();
    let max_rate = config.max_sample_rate();
    let sample_rate = if min_rate <= 48_000 && max_rate >= 48_000 {
        48_000
    } else if min_rate <= 44_100 && max_rate >= 44_100 {
        44_100
    } else {
        max_rate
    };
    Some(config.with_sample_rate(sample_rate))
}

pub struct AudioEngine {
    pub backend: AudioBackend,
    pub cmd_tx: Sender<AudioCommand>,
    pub pos_rx: Receiver<PositionUpdate>,
    pub output_devices: Vec<String>,
    pub input_devices: Vec<String>,
    pub current_device: String,
    pub current_input_device: String,
    pub output_channels: usize,
    output_device_channels: HashMap<String, usize>,
    output_device_channel_layouts: HashMap<String, Vec<usize>>,
    output_channel_settings: Arc<RwLock<Vec<OutputChannelSetting>>>,
}

impl AudioEngine {
    pub fn new() -> Result<Self> {
        Self::new_with_backend(AudioBackend::Wasapi)
    }

    pub fn new_with_backend(backend: AudioBackend) -> Result<Self> {
        let host = host_for_backend(backend)?;
        let output_devices = Self::list_output_devices(&host);
        let (output_device_channels, output_device_channel_layouts) =
            Self::list_output_device_info(&host);
        let input_devices = Self::list_input_devices(&host);

        let out_dev = host
            .default_output_device()
            .or_else(|| {
                host.output_devices()
                    .ok()
                    .and_then(|mut devices| devices.next())
            })
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                anyhow::anyhow!("No output audio device found for {}", backend.label())
            })?;
        let out_name = out_dev
            .name()
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| "Default Output".to_string());
        let output_config = preferred_output_config(&out_dev)
            .or_else(|| out_dev.default_output_config().ok())
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                anyhow::anyhow!("No supported output configuration found for {}", out_name)
            })?;
        let output_channels = output_config.channels() as usize;

        let in_name = host
            .default_input_device()
            .and_then(|d| d.name().ok())
            .unwrap_or_else(|| "Default Input".to_string());

        info!(
            "Audio backend: {}; Output: {}, Input: {}",
            backend.label(),
            out_name,
            in_name
        );

        let (cmd_tx, cmd_rx) = bounded::<AudioCommand>(256);
        let (pos_tx, pos_rx) = bounded::<PositionUpdate>(1024);
        let output_channel_settings = Arc::new(RwLock::new(vec![
            OutputChannelSetting::default();
            output_channels.max(1)
        ]));
        let worker_output_settings = output_channel_settings.clone();
        let worker_default_device = out_name.clone();

        std::thread::spawn(move || {
            audio_worker(
                backend,
                out_dev,
                output_config,
                worker_default_device,
                cmd_rx,
                pos_tx,
                worker_output_settings,
            );
        });

        Ok(Self {
            backend,
            cmd_tx,
            pos_rx,
            output_devices,
            input_devices,
            current_device: out_name,
            current_input_device: in_name,
            output_channels,
            output_device_channels,
            output_device_channel_layouts,
            output_channel_settings,
        })
    }

    fn list_output_devices(host: &Host) -> Vec<String> {
        host.output_devices()
            .map(|devs| devs.filter_map(|d| d.name().ok()).collect())
            .unwrap_or_default()
    }

    fn list_output_device_info(
        host: &Host,
    ) -> (HashMap<String, usize>, HashMap<String, Vec<usize>>) {
        let mut max_channels = HashMap::new();
        let mut layouts_by_device = HashMap::new();
        if let Ok(devices) = host.output_devices() {
            for device in devices {
                let Ok(name) = device.name() else {
                    continue;
                };
                let layouts = supported_output_channel_layouts(&device);
                let channels = layouts
                    .last()
                    .copied()
                    .or_else(|| {
                        device
                            .default_output_config()
                            .ok()
                            .map(|config| config.channels() as usize)
                    })
                    .unwrap_or(2)
                    .max(1);
                info!(
                    "Output device '{}' reports channel layouts: {:?}; selected maximum: {}",
                    name, layouts, channels
                );
                max_channels.insert(name.clone(), channels);
                layouts_by_device.insert(name, layouts);
            }
        }
        (max_channels, layouts_by_device)
    }

    /// Refresh the channel layouts for a device when it is selected. This avoids
    /// keeping stale channel counts if the Windows endpoint configuration changed
    /// after CueLabs started.
    pub fn refresh_output_device_info(&mut self, device_name: &str) {
        let Ok(host) = host_for_backend(self.backend) else {
            return;
        };
        let Ok(devices) = host.output_devices() else {
            return;
        };
        let Some(device) = devices
            .into_iter()
            .find(|device| device.name().ok().as_deref() == Some(device_name))
        else {
            return;
        };
        let layouts = supported_output_channel_layouts(&device);
        let channels = layouts
            .last()
            .copied()
            .or_else(|| {
                device
                    .default_output_config()
                    .ok()
                    .map(|config| config.channels() as usize)
            })
            .unwrap_or(2)
            .max(1);
        info!(
            "Refreshed output device '{}' channel layouts: {:?}; max={}",
            device_name, layouts, channels
        );
        self.output_device_channels
            .insert(device_name.to_string(), channels);
        self.output_device_channel_layouts
            .insert(device_name.to_string(), layouts);
        if self.current_device == device_name {
            self.output_channels = channels;
            let mut settings = self.output_channel_settings.write();
            settings.resize(channels, OutputChannelSetting::default());
            settings.truncate(channels);
        }
    }

    pub fn output_channel_layouts_for_device(&self, device_name: Option<&str>) -> Vec<usize> {
        let name = device_name.unwrap_or(self.current_device.as_str());
        self.output_device_channel_layouts
            .get(name)
            .cloned()
            .unwrap_or_default()
    }

    /// Physical output count for a selected cue device. `None` means global default.
    pub fn output_channels_for_device(&self, device_name: Option<&str>) -> usize {
        match device_name {
            Some(name) => self
                .output_device_channels
                .get(name)
                .copied()
                .unwrap_or(self.output_channels)
                .max(1),
            None => self.output_channels.max(1),
        }
    }

    fn list_input_devices(host: &Host) -> Vec<String> {
        host.input_devices()
            .map(|devs| devs.filter_map(|d| d.name().ok()).collect())
            .unwrap_or_default()
    }

    pub fn set_output_device(&mut self, name: &str) {
        self.refresh_output_device_info(name);
        self.output_channels = self
            .output_device_channels
            .get(name)
            .copied()
            .unwrap_or(2)
            .max(1);
        {
            let mut settings = self.output_channel_settings.write();
            settings.resize(self.output_channels, OutputChannelSetting::default());
            settings.truncate(self.output_channels);
        }
        self.current_device = name.to_string();
        self.send(AudioCommand::SetOutputDevice(name.to_string()));
    }

    pub fn output_channel_settings(&self) -> Vec<OutputChannelSetting> {
        self.output_channel_settings.read().clone()
    }

    pub fn set_output_channel_settings(&self, settings: Vec<OutputChannelSetting>) {
        let mut current = self.output_channel_settings.write();
        *current = settings;
        current.resize(self.output_channels.max(1), OutputChannelSetting::default());
        current.truncate(self.output_channels.max(1));
    }

    pub fn set_input_device(&mut self, name: &str) {
        self.current_input_device = name.to_string();
        self.send(AudioCommand::SetInputDevice(name.to_string()));
    }

    pub fn send(&self, cmd: AudioCommand) {
        if let Err(e) = self.cmd_tx.send(cmd) {
            error!("Failed to send audio command: {}", e);
        }
    }

    pub fn drain_position_updates(&self) -> Vec<PositionUpdate> {
        let mut updates = Vec::new();
        while let Ok(u) = self.pos_rx.try_recv() {
            updates.push(u);
        }
        updates
    }
}

impl Drop for AudioEngine {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let _ = self.cmd_tx.send(AudioCommand::Shutdown);
    }
}

struct DynamicFade {
    start_vol: f32,
    target_vol: f32,
    start_time: Instant,
    duration_secs: f64,
    stop_on_finish: bool,
    curve: FadeCurve,
    custom_fade_points: Vec<[f32; 2]>,
}

struct ActivePlayer {
    id: Uuid,
    file: PathBuf,
    sink: rodio::Player,
    start_secs: f64,
    end_secs: f64,
    base_volume: f32,
    current_volume: f32,
    fade_in_secs: f64,
    fade_out_secs: f64,
    fade_curve: FadeCurve,
    custom_fade_points: Vec<[f32; 2]>,
    channel_routes: Arc<RwLock<Vec<ChannelRoute>>>,
    output_channels: usize,
    output_device: String,
    uses_global_default: bool,
    mixer: rodio::mixer::Mixer,
    output_settings: Arc<RwLock<Vec<OutputChannelSetting>>>,
    loop_enabled: bool,
    loop_start: f64,
    loop_end: f64,
    loops_remaining: i32, // -1 = inf
    segment_start_pos: f64,
    segment_instant: Instant,
    paused_at: Option<f64>,
    dynamic_fade: Option<DynamicFade>,
}

impl ActivePlayer {
    fn current_pos(&self) -> f64 {
        if let Some(p) = self.paused_at {
            p
        } else {
            self.segment_start_pos + self.segment_instant.elapsed().as_secs_f64()
        }
    }
}

fn db_to_linear(db: f32) -> f32 {
    if db <= -60.0 {
        0.0
    } else {
        10.0_f32.powf(db / 20.0)
    }
}

struct ChannelRouter<S> {
    source: S,
    input_channels: usize,
    output_channels: usize,
    output_index: usize,
    input_frame: Vec<f32>,
    output_frame: Vec<f32>,
    routes: Arc<RwLock<Vec<ChannelRoute>>>,
    output_settings: Arc<RwLock<Vec<OutputChannelSetting>>>,
}

impl<S> ChannelRouter<S>
where
    S: Source,
{
    fn new(
        source: S,
        routes: Arc<RwLock<Vec<ChannelRoute>>>,
        output_settings: Arc<RwLock<Vec<OutputChannelSetting>>>,
        output_channels: usize,
    ) -> Self {
        let input_channels = source.channels().get().max(1) as usize;
        let output_channels = output_channels.max(1);

        // Keep the route table aligned with the decoder's real channel count.
        // This also provides a deterministic default map for legacy projects
        // whose per-track route list was empty or shorter than the audio stream.
        {
            let mut route_list = routes.write();
            route_list.truncate(input_channels);
            while route_list.len() < input_channels {
                let source_index = route_list.len();
                route_list.push(ChannelRoute {
                    active: true,
                    output_channel: source_index.min(output_channels - 1),
                    gain_db: 0.0,
                });
            }
            for route in route_list.iter_mut() {
                route.output_channel = route.output_channel.min(output_channels - 1);
            }
            info!(
                "ChannelRouter configured: {} source channels -> {} hardware outputs; routes={:?}",
                input_channels, output_channels, &*route_list
            );
        }

        Self {
            source,
            input_channels,
            output_channels,
            output_index: output_channels,
            input_frame: vec![0.0; input_channels],
            output_frame: vec![0.0; output_channels],
            routes,
            output_settings,
        }
    }
}

impl<S> Iterator for ChannelRouter<S>
where
    S: Source,
{
    type Item = f32;

    fn next(&mut self) -> Option<Self::Item> {
        if self.output_index >= self.output_channels {
            for sample in &mut self.input_frame {
                *sample = self.source.next()?;
            }
            self.output_frame.fill(0.0);

            let routes = self.routes.read();
            let outputs = self.output_settings.read();
            for (source_index, sample) in self.input_frame.iter().copied().enumerate() {
                let fallback_route = ChannelRoute {
                    active: true,
                    output_channel: source_index.min(self.output_channels - 1),
                    gain_db: 0.0,
                };
                let route = routes.get(source_index).unwrap_or(&fallback_route);
                if !route.active || route.output_channel >= self.output_channels {
                    continue;
                }

                let output_setting = outputs.get(route.output_channel);
                if output_setting
                    .map(|setting| !setting.active)
                    .unwrap_or(false)
                {
                    continue;
                }
                let output_gain = output_setting
                    .map(|setting| db_to_linear(setting.gain_db))
                    .unwrap_or(1.0);
                self.output_frame[route.output_channel] +=
                    sample * db_to_linear(route.gain_db) * output_gain;
            }
            self.output_index = 0;
        }

        let sample = self.output_frame[self.output_index];
        self.output_index += 1;
        Some(sample)
    }

    fn size_hint(&self) -> (usize, Option<usize>) {
        let (lower, upper) = self.source.size_hint();
        let pending = self.output_channels.saturating_sub(self.output_index);
        let lower_frames = lower / self.input_channels;
        let lower = lower_frames
            .saturating_mul(self.output_channels)
            .saturating_add(pending);
        let upper = upper.map(|samples| {
            (samples / self.input_channels)
                .saturating_mul(self.output_channels)
                .saturating_add(pending)
        });
        (lower, upper)
    }
}

impl<S> Source for ChannelRouter<S>
where
    S: Source,
{
    fn current_span_len(&self) -> Option<usize> {
        self.source.current_span_len().map(|samples| {
            (samples / self.input_channels)
                .saturating_mul(self.output_channels)
                .saturating_add(self.output_channels.saturating_sub(self.output_index))
        })
    }

    fn channels(&self) -> std::num::NonZeroU16 {
        std::num::NonZeroU16::new(self.output_channels.min(u16::MAX as usize) as u16)
            .expect("ChannelRouter always has at least one output channel")
    }

    fn sample_rate(&self) -> std::num::NonZeroU32 {
        self.source.sample_rate()
    }

    fn total_duration(&self) -> Option<Duration> {
        self.source.total_duration()
    }
}

fn open_sink_at(
    mixer: &rodio::mixer::Mixer,
    file_path: &PathBuf,
    offset_secs: f64,
    initial_vol: f32,
    output_channels: usize,
    routes: Arc<RwLock<Vec<ChannelRoute>>>,
    output_settings: Arc<RwLock<Vec<OutputChannelSetting>>>,
) -> Option<rodio::Player> {
    let file = std::fs::File::open(file_path).ok()?;
    let player = rodio::Player::connect_new(mixer);
    let decoder = rodio::Decoder::try_from(file).ok()?;
    let source = decoder.skip_duration(Duration::from_secs_f64(offset_secs.max(0.0)));
    let routed = ChannelRouter::new(source, routes, output_settings, output_channels);
    player.set_volume(initial_vol);
    player.append(routed);
    player.play();
    Some(player)
}

struct DeviceOutput {
    // Keeping the stream alive is required for the device's output callback.
    _stream: rodio::MixerDeviceSink,
    mixer: rodio::mixer::Mixer,
    channels: usize,
    settings: Arc<RwLock<Vec<OutputChannelSetting>>>,
}

fn build_device_output(
    device: &Device,
    config: cpal::SupportedStreamConfig,
    device_name: &str,
    default_device_name: &str,
    default_settings: &Arc<RwLock<Vec<OutputChannelSetting>>>,
) -> Option<DeviceOutput> {
    let channels = (config.channels() as usize).max(1);
    let sample_rate = config.sample_rate();
    let sample_format = config.sample_format();
    info!(
        "Opening output '{}' with {} channels @ {} Hz ({:?})",
        device_name, channels, sample_rate, sample_format
    );
    let mut stream = rodio::DeviceSinkBuilder::default()
        .with_device(device.clone())
        .with_supported_config(&config)
        .open_stream()
        .ok()?;
    stream.log_on_drop(false);
    let mixer = stream.mixer().clone();
    let settings = if device_name == default_device_name {
        default_settings.clone()
    } else {
        Arc::new(RwLock::new(vec![OutputChannelSetting::default(); channels]))
    };
    Some(DeviceOutput {
        _stream: stream,
        mixer,
        channels,
        settings,
    })
}

fn open_device_output(
    device_name: &str,
    host: &Host,
    default_device_name: &str,
    default_settings: &Arc<RwLock<Vec<OutputChannelSetting>>>,
) -> Option<DeviceOutput> {
    let devices = host.output_devices().ok()?;
    let device = devices
        .into_iter()
        .find(|device| device.name().ok().as_deref() == Some(device_name))?;
    let config =
        preferred_output_config(&device).or_else(|| device.default_output_config().ok())?;
    build_device_output(
        &device,
        config,
        device_name,
        default_device_name,
        default_settings,
    )
}

fn ensure_device_output(
    streams: &mut HashMap<String, DeviceOutput>,
    device_name: &str,
    host: &Host,
    default_device_name: &str,
    default_settings: &Arc<RwLock<Vec<OutputChannelSetting>>>,
) -> Option<(
    rodio::mixer::Mixer,
    usize,
    Arc<RwLock<Vec<OutputChannelSetting>>>,
)> {
    if !streams.contains_key(device_name) {
        let output = open_device_output(device_name, host, default_device_name, default_settings)?;
        streams.insert(device_name.to_string(), output);
    }
    let output = streams.get_mut(device_name)?;
    if device_name == default_device_name {
        output.settings = default_settings.clone();
    }
    Some((
        output.mixer.clone(),
        output.channels,
        output.settings.clone(),
    ))
}

fn audio_worker(
    backend: AudioBackend,
    initial_device: Device,
    initial_config: cpal::SupportedStreamConfig,
    initial_device_name: String,
    cmd_rx: Receiver<AudioCommand>,
    pos_tx: Sender<PositionUpdate>,
    output_settings: Arc<RwLock<Vec<OutputChannelSetting>>>,
) {
    let host = match host_for_backend(backend) {
        Ok(host) => host,
        Err(error) => {
            error!(
                "Could not initialise {} audio host in worker: {}",
                backend.label(),
                error
            );
            return;
        }
    };
    let Some(initial_output) = build_device_output(
        &initial_device,
        initial_config,
        &initial_device_name,
        &initial_device_name,
        &output_settings,
    ) else {
        error!(
            "Could not open initial {} audio output stream",
            backend.label()
        );
        return;
    };

    let mut streams = HashMap::new();
    streams.insert(initial_device_name.clone(), initial_output);
    let mut default_device_name = initial_device_name;
    let mut players: Vec<ActivePlayer> = Vec::new();
    let tick = Duration::from_millis(5);

    loop {
        while let Ok(cmd) = cmd_rx.try_recv() {
            match cmd {
                AudioCommand::Shutdown => {
                    for player in &players {
                        player.sink.stop();
                    }
                    players.clear();
                    return;
                }
                AudioCommand::SetOutputDevice(dev_name) => {
                    // Stop cues that follow the global default, but leave cues pinned
                    // to an explicit per-cue device running on their own streams.
                    players.retain(|player| {
                        if player.uses_global_default {
                            info!(
                                "Stopping cue {} on previous global output '{}'",
                                player.id, player.output_device
                            );
                            player.sink.stop();
                            false
                        } else {
                            true
                        }
                    });
                    default_device_name = dev_name.clone();
                    if let Some((_, channels, settings)) = ensure_device_output(
                        &mut streams,
                        &dev_name,
                        &host,
                        &default_device_name,
                        &output_settings,
                    ) {
                        let mut current = settings.write();
                        current.resize(channels, OutputChannelSetting::default());
                        current.truncate(channels);
                        info!(
                            "Switched global output device to: {} ({} channels)",
                            dev_name, channels
                        );
                    } else {
                        warn!(
                            "Could not open selected output configuration for device: {}",
                            dev_name
                        );
                    }
                }
                AudioCommand::SetInputDevice(dev_name) => {
                    info!("Selected input audio device: {}", dev_name);
                }
                other => handle_command(
                    other,
                    &mut players,
                    &mut streams,
                    &host,
                    &default_device_name,
                    &output_settings,
                ),
            }
        }

        let mut finished_ids = Vec::new();
        for player in &mut players {
            if player.sink.is_paused() {
                let _ = pos_tx.try_send(PositionUpdate {
                    id: player.id,
                    position_secs: player.current_pos(),
                    state: CueState::Paused,
                });
                continue;
            }

            let mut pos = player.current_pos();

            // Loop restart must use the same output device as the original cue.
            let valid_loop = player.loop_enabled && player.loop_end > player.loop_start;
            if valid_loop && pos >= player.loop_end {
                if player.loops_remaining == -1 || player.loops_remaining > 1 {
                    if player.loops_remaining > 1 {
                        player.loops_remaining -= 1;
                    }
                    player.sink.stop();
                    if let Some(new_sink) = open_sink_at(
                        &player.mixer,
                        &player.file,
                        player.loop_start,
                        player.current_volume,
                        player.output_channels,
                        player.channel_routes.clone(),
                        player.output_settings.clone(),
                    ) {
                        player.sink = new_sink;
                        player.segment_start_pos = player.loop_start;
                        player.segment_instant = Instant::now();
                        pos = player.loop_start;
                    } else {
                        finished_ids.push(player.id);
                        continue;
                    }
                }
            }

            if (player.end_secs > 0.0 && pos >= player.end_secs) || player.sink.empty() {
                player.sink.stop();
                finished_ids.push(player.id);
                continue;
            }

            let mut state = CueState::Playing;
            let mut env = 1.0_f32;
            let elapsed_from_start = (pos - player.start_secs).max(0.0);
            if player.fade_in_secs > 0.0 && elapsed_from_start < player.fade_in_secs {
                let t = (elapsed_from_start / player.fade_in_secs) as f32;
                env *= player
                    .fade_curve
                    .apply_with_points(t, &player.custom_fade_points);
                state = CueState::FadingIn;
            }
            if player.end_secs > 0.0 && player.fade_out_secs > 0.0 {
                let remaining = (player.end_secs - pos).max(0.0);
                if remaining < player.fade_out_secs {
                    let t = (remaining / player.fade_out_secs) as f32;
                    env *= player
                        .fade_curve
                        .apply_with_points(t, &player.custom_fade_points);
                    state = CueState::FadingOut;
                }
            }

            if let Some(ref fade) = player.dynamic_fade {
                let elapsed = fade.start_time.elapsed().as_secs_f64();
                if elapsed >= fade.duration_secs {
                    player.base_volume = fade.target_vol;
                    let should_stop = fade.stop_on_finish;
                    player.dynamic_fade = None;
                    if should_stop {
                        player.sink.stop();
                        finished_ids.push(player.id);
                        continue;
                    }
                } else {
                    let t = (elapsed / fade.duration_secs.max(0.001)) as f32;
                    let shaped = fade.curve.apply_with_points(t, &fade.custom_fade_points);
                    let interpolated = fade.start_vol + (fade.target_vol - fade.start_vol) * shaped;
                    player.current_volume = interpolated * env;
                    player.sink.set_volume(player.current_volume);
                    state = if fade.target_vol >= fade.start_vol {
                        CueState::FadingIn
                    } else {
                        CueState::FadingOut
                    };
                    let _ = pos_tx.try_send(PositionUpdate {
                        id: player.id,
                        position_secs: pos,
                        state,
                    });
                    continue;
                }
            }

            player.current_volume = player.base_volume * env;
            player.sink.set_volume(player.current_volume);
            let _ = pos_tx.try_send(PositionUpdate {
                id: player.id,
                position_secs: pos,
                state,
            });
        }

        for finished_id in finished_ids {
            players.retain(|player| player.id != finished_id);
            let _ = pos_tx.try_send(PositionUpdate {
                id: finished_id,
                position_secs: 0.0,
                state: CueState::Stopped,
            });
        }

        std::thread::sleep(tick);
    }
}

fn handle_command(
    cmd: AudioCommand,
    players: &mut Vec<ActivePlayer>,
    streams: &mut HashMap<String, DeviceOutput>,
    host: &Host,
    default_device_name: &str,
    default_settings: &Arc<RwLock<Vec<OutputChannelSetting>>>,
) {
    match cmd {
        AudioCommand::Play {
            id,
            file,
            output_device,
            start_secs,
            end_secs,
            duration_secs,
            volume,
            fade_in_secs,
            fade_out_secs,
            fade_curve,
            custom_fade_points,
            channel_routes,
            loop_enabled,
            loop_start,
            loop_end,
            loop_count,
        } => {
            let prepare_started = Instant::now();
            players.retain(|player| {
                if player.id == id {
                    player.sink.stop();
                    false
                } else {
                    true
                }
            });

            let uses_global_default = output_device.is_none();
            let device_name = output_device.unwrap_or_else(|| default_device_name.to_string());
            let Some((mixer, output_channels, output_settings)) = ensure_device_output(
                streams,
                &device_name,
                host,
                default_device_name,
                default_settings,
            ) else {
                warn!("Cannot open output device '{}' for cue {}", device_name, id);
                return;
            };

            let initial_volume = if fade_in_secs > 0.0 { 0.0 } else { volume };
            let route_state = Arc::new(RwLock::new(channel_routes));
            let Some(sink) = open_sink_at(
                &mixer,
                &file,
                start_secs,
                initial_volume,
                output_channels,
                route_state.clone(),
                output_settings.clone(),
            ) else {
                warn!("Cannot open audio file for playback: {:?}", file);
                return;
            };
            info!(
                "Cue {} output device + decoder/sink prepared in {:.1} ms (device: {})",
                id,
                prepare_started.elapsed().as_secs_f64() * 1000.0,
                device_name
            );

            let effective_end = end_secs
                .unwrap_or(duration_secs)
                .min(if duration_secs > 0.0 {
                    duration_secs
                } else {
                    f64::MAX
                });
            let effective_loop_end = if loop_end > loop_start {
                loop_end
            } else {
                effective_end
            };

            players.push(ActivePlayer {
                id,
                file,
                sink,
                start_secs,
                end_secs: if effective_end == f64::MAX {
                    0.0
                } else {
                    effective_end
                },
                base_volume: volume,
                current_volume: initial_volume,
                fade_in_secs,
                fade_out_secs,
                fade_curve,
                custom_fade_points,
                channel_routes: route_state,
                output_channels,
                output_device: device_name,
                uses_global_default,
                mixer,
                output_settings,
                loop_enabled,
                loop_start,
                loop_end: effective_loop_end,
                loops_remaining: loop_count,
                segment_start_pos: start_secs,
                segment_instant: Instant::now(),
                paused_at: None,
                dynamic_fade: None,
            });
        }

        AudioCommand::Stop(id) => {
            if let Some(player) = players.iter().find(|player| player.id == id) {
                player.sink.stop();
            }
            players.retain(|player| player.id != id);
        }

        AudioCommand::StopAll => {
            for player in players.iter() {
                player.sink.stop();
            }
            players.clear();
        }

        AudioCommand::Pause(id) => {
            if let Some(player) = players.iter_mut().find(|player| player.id == id) {
                let position = player.current_pos();
                player.sink.pause();
                player.paused_at = Some(position);
            }
        }

        AudioCommand::Seek { id, position_secs } => {
            if let Some(player) = players.iter_mut().find(|player| player.id == id) {
                let min_position = player.start_secs.max(0.0);
                let max_position = if player.end_secs > 0.0 {
                    (player.end_secs - 0.01).max(min_position)
                } else {
                    f64::MAX
                };
                let position = position_secs.clamp(min_position, max_position);
                player.sink.stop();
                if let Some(new_sink) = open_sink_at(
                    &player.mixer,
                    &player.file,
                    position,
                    player.current_volume,
                    player.output_channels,
                    player.channel_routes.clone(),
                    player.output_settings.clone(),
                ) {
                    new_sink.pause();
                    player.sink = new_sink;
                    player.segment_start_pos = position;
                    player.segment_instant = Instant::now();
                    player.paused_at = Some(position);
                    player.dynamic_fade = None;
                } else {
                    warn!("Could not seek audio cue {} to {:.3}s", id, position);
                }
            }
        }

        AudioCommand::PauseAll => {
            for player in players.iter_mut() {
                let position = player.current_pos();
                player.sink.pause();
                player.paused_at = Some(position);
            }
        }

        AudioCommand::Resume(id) => {
            if let Some(player) = players.iter_mut().find(|player| player.id == id) {
                if let Some(position) = player.paused_at.take() {
                    player.segment_start_pos = position;
                    player.segment_instant = Instant::now();
                }
                player.sink.play();
            }
        }

        AudioCommand::ResumeAll => {
            for player in players.iter_mut() {
                if let Some(position) = player.paused_at.take() {
                    player.segment_start_pos = position;
                    player.segment_instant = Instant::now();
                }
                player.sink.play();
            }
        }

        AudioCommand::SetVolume(id, volume) => {
            if let Some(player) = players.iter_mut().find(|player| player.id == id) {
                player.base_volume = volume;
                player.sink.set_volume(volume);
            }
        }

        AudioCommand::Fade {
            id,
            target_volume,
            duration_secs,
            stop_on_finish,
            curve,
            custom_fade_points,
        } => {
            if let Some(player) = players.iter_mut().find(|player| player.id == id) {
                player.dynamic_fade = Some(DynamicFade {
                    start_vol: player.current_volume,
                    target_vol: target_volume,
                    start_time: Instant::now(),
                    duration_secs,
                    stop_on_finish,
                    curve,
                    custom_fade_points,
                });
            }
        }

        AudioCommand::SetRouting { id, routes } => {
            if let Some(player) = players.iter_mut().find(|player| player.id == id) {
                *player.channel_routes.write() = routes;
            }
        }

        AudioCommand::SetOutputDevice(_)
        | AudioCommand::SetInputDevice(_)
        | AudioCommand::Shutdown => {}
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod channel_router_tests {
    use super::*;
    use rodio::buffer::SamplesBuffer;

    #[test]
    fn track_routes_map_source_channels_to_selected_hardware_outputs() {
        let routes = Arc::new(RwLock::new(vec![
            ChannelRoute {
                active: true,
                output_channel: 2,
                gain_db: 0.0,
            },
            ChannelRoute {
                active: true,
                output_channel: 3,
                gain_db: 0.0,
            },
        ]));
        let output_settings = Arc::new(RwLock::new(vec![OutputChannelSetting::default(); 4]));
        let source = SamplesBuffer::new(
            std::num::NonZeroU16::new(2).unwrap(),
            std::num::NonZeroU32::new(48_000).unwrap(),
            vec![0.25_f32, 0.75_f32],
        );
        let router = ChannelRouter::new(source, routes, output_settings, 4);
        let output: Vec<f32> = router.take(4).collect();

        assert_eq!(output, vec![0.0, 0.0, 0.25, 0.75]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn inactive_track_source_channel_is_not_sent() {
        let routes = Arc::new(RwLock::new(vec![
            ChannelRoute {
                active: false,
                output_channel: 0,
                gain_db: 0.0,
            },
            ChannelRoute {
                active: true,
                output_channel: 1,
                gain_db: 0.0,
            },
        ]));
        let output_settings = Arc::new(RwLock::new(vec![OutputChannelSetting::default(); 2]));
        let source = SamplesBuffer::new(
            std::num::NonZeroU16::new(2).unwrap(),
            std::num::NonZeroU32::new(48_000).unwrap(),
            vec![0.5_f32, 0.75_f32],
        );
        let router = ChannelRouter::new(source, routes, output_settings, 2);
        let output: Vec<f32> = router.take(2).collect();

        assert_eq!(output, vec![0.0, 0.75]);
    }
}

// === FILE: D:\winlab\cuelabs\src\audio\waveform.rs ===

// src/audio/waveform.rs вЂ” РіРµРЅРµСЂР°С†РёСЏ РїРёРєРѕРІ РІРµР№РІС„РѕСЂРјС‹ Рё С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕРµ РѕРїСЂРµРґРµР»РµРЅРёРµ РґР»РёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё

use rodio::Source;
use std::path::Path;
use tracing::{debug, error};

/// РЎР¶РёРјР°РµС‚ Р°СѓРґРёРѕС„Р°Р№Р» РІ РјР°СЃСЃРёРІ РїР°СЂ [min, max] РґР»СЏ РѕС‚СЂРёСЃРѕРІРєРё РІРµР№РІС„РѕСЂРјС‹
pub fn generate_waveform(path: &Path, buckets: usize) -> Vec<f32> {
    if path.as_os_str().is_empty() {
        return vec![];
    }
    let file = match std::fs::File::open(path) {
        Ok(f) => f,
        Err(e) => {
            error!("Waveform: cannot open {:?}: {e}", path);
            return vec![0.0; buckets * 2];
        }
    };

    let decoder = match rodio::Decoder::new(std::io::BufReader::new(file)) {
        Ok(d) => d,
        Err(e) => {
            error!("Waveform: decode error {:?}: {e}", path);
            return vec![0.0; buckets * 2];
        }
    };

    let samples: Vec<f32> = decoder.map(|s| s as f32 / i16::MAX as f32).collect();

    if samples.is_empty() {
        return vec![0.0; buckets * 2];
    }

    let bucket_size = (samples.len() / buckets).max(1);
    let mut result = Vec::with_capacity(buckets * 2);

    for i in 0..buckets {
        let start = i * bucket_size;
        let end = ((i + 1) * bucket_size).min(samples.len());
        if start >= samples.len() {
            result.push(0.0);
            result.push(0.0);
            continue;
        }
        let slice = &samples[start..end];
        let min = slice.iter().copied().fold(f32::INFINITY, f32::min);
        let max = slice.iter().copied().fold(f32::NEG_INFINITY, f32::max);
        result.push(min.clamp(-1.0, 0.0));
        result.push(max.clamp(0.0, 1.0));
    }

    debug!("Waveform generated: {} buckets for {:?}", buckets, path);
    result
}

/// РўРѕС‡РЅРѕРµ РѕРїСЂРµРґРµР»РµРЅРёРµ РґР»РёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё Р°СѓРґРёРѕС„Р°Р№Р»Р° СЃ СѓС‡РµС‚РѕРј С‡РёСЃР»Р° РєР°РЅР°Р»РѕРІ Рё С‡Р°СЃС‚РѕС‚С‹ РґРёСЃРєСЂРµС‚РёР·Р°С†РёРё
pub fn get_duration(path: &Path) -> f64 {
    if path.as_os_str().is_empty() {
        return 0.0;
    }
    let file = match std::fs::File::open(path) {
        Ok(f) => f,
        Err(_) => return 0.0,
    };
    match rodio::Decoder::new(std::io::BufReader::new(file)) {
        Ok(d) => {
            if let Some(dur) = d.total_duration() {
                return dur.as_secs_f64();
            }
            let channels = d.channels().get().max(1) as f64;
            let rate = d.sample_rate().get().max(1) as f64;
            let total_samples = d.count() as f64;
            total_samples / (channels * rate)
        }
        Err(_) => 0.0,
    }
}

/// Returns the number of interleaved audio channels reported by the decoder.
pub fn get_channel_count(path: &Path) -> usize {
    if path.as_os_str().is_empty() {
        return 0;
    }
    let Ok(file) = std::fs::File::open(path) else {
        return 0;
    };
    rodio::Decoder::new(std::io::BufReader::new(file))
        .map(|decoder| decoder.channels().get().max(1) as usize)
        .unwrap_or(0)
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum MidiControlKind {
    NoteOn,
    ControlChange,
}

impl MidiControlKind {
    pub fn label(&self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::NoteOn => "Note",
            Self::ControlChange => "CC",
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct MidiControlBinding {
    /// MIDI channel in the user-facing 1..=16 range.
    pub channel: u8,
    pub kind: MidiControlKind,
    /// Note number for NoteOn, or controller number for ControlChange.
    pub number: u8,
    pub action: AppAction,
}

impl MidiControlBinding {
    pub fn label(&self) -> String {
        format!(
            "Ch {} В· {} {}",
            self.channel,
            self.kind.label(),
            self.number
        )
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize, Default)]
pub enum AudioBackend {
    /// Windows shared-mode audio through the system audio engine.
    #[default]
    Wasapi,
    /// Windows low-latency direct hardware backend. Requires an installed ASIO driver.
    Asio,
}

impl AudioBackend {
    pub const ALL: &'static [AudioBackend] = &[AudioBackend::Wasapi, AudioBackend::Asio];

    pub fn label(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            AudioBackend::Wasapi => {
                if cfg!(target_os = "windows") {
                    "WASAPI (Shared)"
                } else {
                    "System Default"
                }
            }
            AudioBackend::Asio => "ASIO",
        }
    }
}

        bindings.insert(AppAction::ResumeAll, "Shift+R".to_string());
        bindings.insert(AppAction::SelectNext, "Down".to_string());
        bindings.insert(AppAction::SelectPrev, "Up".to_string());
        bindings.insert(AppAction::SaveProject, "Ctrl+S".to_string());
        bindings.insert(AppAction::OpenProject, "Ctrl+O".to_string());
        bindings.insert(AppAction::NewProject, "Ctrl+N".to_string());
        bindings.insert(AppAction::AddFile, "Ctrl+I".to_string());
        bindings.insert(AppAction::DeleteSelected, "Delete".to_string());
        bindings.insert(AppAction::RenameSelected, "F2".to_string());
        bindings.insert(AppAction::MoveUp, "Alt+Up".to_string());
        bindings.insert(AppAction::MoveDown, "Alt+Down".to_string());
        Self { bindings }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(default)]
pub struct AppSettings {
    pub hotkeys: HotkeyConfig,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub audio_backend: AudioBackend,
    pub audio_output_device: Option<String>,
    pub audio_input_device: Option<String>,
    pub osc_listen_port: u16,
    pub osc_send_addr: String,
    pub midi_input_port: Option<String>,
    pub midi_output_port: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub midi_controls: Vec<MidiControlBinding>,
    pub output_channels: Vec<OutputChannelSetting>,
    pub stop_all_fade_secs: f64,
    /// User-resized Cue List column widths, in logical points.
    #[serde(default = "default_cue_column_widths")]
    pub cue_column_widths: [f32; 9],
    /// User-resized lower inspector panel height, in logical points.
    #[serde(default = "default_bottom_panel_height")]
    pub bottom_panel_height: f32,
}

impl Default for AppSettings {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            hotkeys: HotkeyConfig::default(),
            audio_backend: AudioBackend::Wasapi,
            audio_output_device: None,
            audio_input_device: None,
            osc_listen_port: 7700,
            osc_send_addr: "127.0.0.1:7701".to_string(),
            midi_input_port: None,
            midi_output_port: None,
            midi_controls: Vec::new(),
            output_channels: Vec::new(),
            stop_all_fade_secs: 0.0,
            cue_column_widths: default_cue_column_widths(),
            bottom_panel_height: default_bottom_panel_height(),
        }
    }
}

fn default_cue_column_widths() -> [f32; 9] {
    [44.0, 75.0, 210.0, 165.0, 68.0, 78.0, 68.0, 95.0, 125.0]
}

fn default_bottom_panel_height() -> f32 {
    225.0
}

impl AppSettings {
    fn file_path() -> Option<std::path::PathBuf> {
        dirs::config_dir().map(|dir| dir.join("CueLabs").join("settings.json"))
    }

    pub fn load() -> Self {
        let Some(path) = Self::file_path() else {
            return Self::default();
        };
        std::fs::read_to_string(path)
            .ok()
            .and_then(|raw| serde_json::from_str(&raw).ok())
            .unwrap_or_default()
    }

    pub fn save(&self) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        let path = Self::file_path()
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("Cannot resolve application config directory"))?;
        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
            std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
        }
        std::fs::write(path, serde_json::to_vec_pretty(self)?)?;
        Ok(())
    }
}

use egui::Color32;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::path::PathBuf;
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
pub enum CueType {
    Audio,
    Pause {
        target_id: Option<Uuid>,
    },
    Play {
        target_id: Option<Uuid>,
    },
    Stop {
        target_id: Option<Uuid>,
    },
    Fade {
        target_id: Option<Uuid>,
        fade_in: bool,
        duration_secs: f64,
        stop_on_finish: bool,
        /// None follows the target audio cue's configured level for Fade In, or 0 for Fade Out.
        #[serde(default)]
        target_volume: Option<f32>,
        #[serde(default)]
        fade_curve: FadeCurve,
        #[serde(default)]
        custom_fade_points: Vec<[f32; 2]>,
    },
    Midi {
        channel: u8,
        message: MidiMessageKind,
        raw_hex: String,
    },
    Osc {
        address: String,
        #[serde(default)]
        args: Vec<OscCueArg>,
    },
    Group {
        mode: GroupMode,
        children: Vec<Uuid>,
        #[serde(default)]
        fade_in_secs: f64,
        #[serde(default)]
        fade_out_secs: f64,
    },
}

impl CueType {
    pub fn label(&self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            CueType::Audio => "Audio",
            CueType::Pause { .. } => "Pause",
            CueType::Play { .. } => "Play",
            CueType::Stop { .. } => "Stop",
            CueType::Fade { fade_in, .. } => {
                if *fade_in {
                    "Fade In"
                } else {
                    "Fade Out"
                }
            }
            CueType::Midi { .. } => "MIDI",
            CueType::Osc { .. } => "OSC",
            CueType::Group { .. } => "Group",
        }
    }

    pub fn icon(&self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            CueType::Audio => "рџ”Љ",
            CueType::Pause { .. } => "вЏё",
            CueType::Play { .. } => "в–¶",
            CueType::Stop { .. } => "вЏ№",
            CueType::Fade { fade_in, .. } => {
                if *fade_in {
                    "в†—"
                } else {
                    "в†"
                }
            }
            CueType::Midi { .. } => "рџЋ№",
            CueType::Osc { .. } => "рџ“Ў",
            CueType::Group { .. } => "рџ“Ѓ",
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
pub enum OscCueArg {
    Int(i32),
    Float(f32),
    String(String),
    Bool(bool),
}

impl OscCueArg {
    pub fn label(&self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Int(_) => "Integer",
            Self::Float(_) => "Float",
            Self::String(_) => "String",
            Self::Bool(_) => "Boolean",
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
pub enum MidiMessageKind {
    NoteOn { note: u8, velocity: u8 },
    NoteOff { note: u8, velocity: u8 },
    ControlChange { cc: u8, value: u8 },
    ProgramChange { program: u8 },
    PitchBend { value: i16 },
    Raw(Vec<u8>),
}

impl MidiMessageKind {
    pub fn label(&self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            MidiMessageKind::NoteOn { .. } => "Note On",
            MidiMessageKind::NoteOff { .. } => "Note Off",
            MidiMessageKind::ControlChange { .. } => "Control Change",
            MidiMessageKind::ProgramChange { .. } => "Program Change",
            MidiMessageKind::PitchBend { .. } => "Pitch Bend",
            MidiMessageKind::Raw(_) => "Raw MIDI Code",
        }
    }

    pub fn to_bytes(&self, channel: u8) -> Vec<u8> {
        let ch = channel.saturating_sub(1) & 0x0F;
        match self {
            MidiMessageKind::NoteOn { note, velocity } => {
                vec![0x90 | ch, *note & 0x7F, *velocity & 0x7F]
            }
            MidiMessageKind::NoteOff { note, velocity } => {
                vec![0x80 | ch, *note & 0x7F, *velocity & 0x7F]
            }
            MidiMessageKind::ControlChange { cc, value } => {
                vec![0xB0 | ch, *cc & 0x7F, *value & 0x7F]
            }
            MidiMessageKind::ProgramChange { program } => vec![0xC0 | ch, *program & 0x7F],
            MidiMessageKind::PitchBend { value } => {
                let v = (*value + 8192).clamp(0, 16383) as u16;
                vec![0xE0 | ch, (v & 0x7F) as u8, ((v >> 7) & 0x7F) as u8]
            }
            MidiMessageKind::Raw(b) => b.clone(),
        }
    }

    pub fn parse_hex(hex_str: &str) -> Vec<u8> {
        hex_str
            .split(|c: char| c.is_whitespace() || c == ',' || c == ';')
            .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
            .filter_map(|s| {
                let clean = s
                    .strip_prefix("0x")
                    .or_else(|| s.strip_prefix("0X"))
                    .unwrap_or(s);
                u8::from_str_radix(clean, 16).ok()
            })
            .collect()
    }
}

// ==== Р РµР¶РёРјС‹ РІРѕСЃРїСЂРѕРёР·РІРµРґРµРЅРёСЏ РіСЂСѓРїРї ====
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Default)]
pub enum GroupMode {
    /// Р—Р°РїСѓСЃРєР°РµС‚ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РїРµСЂРІРѕРµ СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёРµ. Р”Р°Р»СЊС€Рµ вЂ” РїРѕ auto-continue/follow С†РµРїРѕС‡РєРµ РґРµС‚РµР№.
    #[default]
    Normal,
    /// Plays each child one after another, respecting each child's pre-wait and post-wait.
    Playlist,
    /// Starts every child at once (each child still observes its own pre-wait).
    AllCues,
}

impl GroupMode {
    pub fn label(&self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            GroupMode::Normal => "Normal",
            GroupMode::Playlist => "Playlist",
            GroupMode::AllCues => "All Cues",
        }
    }
    pub const ALL: &'static [GroupMode] =
        &[GroupMode::Normal, GroupMode::Playlist, GroupMode::AllCues];
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Default)]
pub enum ContinueMode {
    #[default]
    Nothing,
    AutoContinue,
    AutoFollow,
}

impl ContinueMode {
    pub fn label(&self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            ContinueMode::Nothing => "Nothing",
            ContinueMode::AutoContinue => "Auto-Continue",
            ContinueMode::AutoFollow => "Auto-Follow",
        }
    }
    pub const ALL: &'static [ContinueMode] = &[
        ContinueMode::Nothing,
        ContinueMode::AutoContinue,
        ContinueMode::AutoFollow,
    ];
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Default)]
pub enum CueState {
    #[default]
    Stopped,
    Waiting,
    Playing,
    FadingIn,
    FadingOut,
    Paused,
    Loading,
    Error(String),
}

impl CueState {
    pub fn is_active(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(
            self,
            CueState::Waiting
                | CueState::Playing
                | CueState::FadingIn
                | CueState::FadingOut
                | CueState::Paused
                | CueState::Loading
        )
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
pub struct ChannelRoute {
    pub active: bool,
    /// Zero-based physical output index. The UI displays it as 1-based.
    pub output_channel: usize,
    pub gain_db: f32,
}

impl Default for ChannelRoute {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            active: true,
            output_channel: 0,
            gain_db: 0.0,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]

    pub active: bool,
    pub gain_db: f32,
}

impl Default for OutputChannelSetting {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            active: true,
            gain_db: 0.0,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
pub struct IoRouting {
    pub output_device: Option<String>,
    pub input_device: Option<String>,
    pub output_channel: u16,
    pub left_gain_db: f32,
    pub right_gain_db: f32,
    pub left_enabled: bool,
    pub right_enabled: bool,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub channel_routes: Vec<ChannelRoute>,
}

impl Default for IoRouting {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            output_device: None,
            input_device: None,
            output_channel: 0,
            left_gain_db: 0.0,
            right_gain_db: 0.0,
            left_enabled: true,
            right_enabled: true,
            channel_routes: Vec::new(),
        }
    }
}

impl IoRouting {
    pub fn ensure_channel_routes(&mut self, input_channels: usize, output_channels: usize) {
        let input_channels = input_channels.max(1);
        let output_channels = output_channels.max(1);
        if self.channel_routes.len() > input_channels {
            self.channel_routes.truncate(input_channels);
        }
        while self.channel_routes.len() < input_channels {
            let index = self.channel_routes.len();
            let (active, gain_db) = match index {
                0 => (self.left_enabled, self.left_gain_db),
                1 => (self.right_enabled, self.right_gain_db),
                _ => (true, 0.0),
            };
            self.channel_routes.push(ChannelRoute {
                active,
                output_channel: if index < output_channels {
                    index
                } else if output_channels == 2 {
                    index % 2
                } else {
                    0
                },
                gain_db,
            });
        }
        for route in &mut self.channel_routes {
            route.output_channel = route.output_channel.min(output_channels - 1);
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Default)]
pub enum FadeCurve {
    #[default]
    Linear,
    SCurve,
    Logarithmic,
    Exponential,
    Custom,
}

impl FadeCurve {
    pub fn apply(&self, t: f32) -> f32 {
        let t = t.clamp(0.0, 1.0);
        match self {
            FadeCurve::Linear => t,
            FadeCurve::SCurve => t * t * (3.0 - 2.0 * t),
            FadeCurve::Logarithmic => (t * 9.0 + 1.0).log10(),
            FadeCurve::Exponential => t * t,
            FadeCurve::Custom => t,
        }
    }

    pub fn apply_with_points(&self, t: f32, points: &[[f32; 2]]) -> f32 {
        let t = t.clamp(0.0, 1.0);
        if *self != FadeCurve::Custom || points.len() < 2 {
            return self.apply(t);
        }

        let mut previous = points[0];
        for next in points.iter().copied().skip(1) {
            if t <= next[0] {
                let span = (next[0] - previous[0]).max(0.0001);
                let local_t = ((t - previous[0]) / span).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
                return (previous[1] + (next[1] - previous[1]) * local_t).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
            }
            previous = next;
        }
        previous[1].clamp(0.0, 1.0)
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Default)]
pub enum CueColor {
    #[default]
    None,
    Red,
    Orange,
    Yellow,
    Green,
    Cyan,
    Blue,
    Purple,
    Pink,
    White,
}

impl CueColor {
    pub fn to_rgb(&self) -> [u8; 3] {
        match self {
            CueColor::None => [60, 60, 75],
            CueColor::Red => [220, 60, 60],
            CueColor::Orange => [220, 140, 40],
            CueColor::Yellow => [220, 210, 40],
            CueColor::Green => [60, 200, 80],
            CueColor::Cyan => [40, 200, 200],
            CueColor::Blue => [60, 130, 230],
            CueColor::Purple => [160, 80, 220],
            CueColor::Pink => [220, 80, 160],
            CueColor::White => [200, 200, 210],
        }
    }

    pub fn to_egui(&self) -> Color32 {
        let [r, g, b] = self.to_rgb();
        Color32::from_rgb(r, g, b)
    }

    /// Р¦РІРµС‚ Р·Р°Р»РёРІРєРё СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРё. Alpha = 26 (~10%).
    pub fn row_bg_egui(&self) -> Option<Color32> {
        if matches!(self, CueColor::None) {
            None
        } else {
            let [r, g, b] = self.to_rgb();
            Some(Color32::from_rgba_unmultiplied(r, g, b, 26))
        }
    }

    pub const ALL: &'static [CueColor] = &[
        CueColor::None,
        CueColor::Red,
        CueColor::Orange,
        CueColor::Yellow,
        CueColor::Green,
        CueColor::Cyan,
        CueColor::Blue,
        CueColor::Purple,
        CueColor::Pink,
        CueColor::White,
    ];

    pub fn label(&self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            CueColor::None => "None",
            CueColor::Red => "Red",
            CueColor::Orange => "Orange",
            CueColor::Yellow => "Yellow",
            CueColor::Green => "Green",
            CueColor::Cyan => "Cyan",
            CueColor::Blue => "Blue",
            CueColor::Purple => "Purple",
            CueColor::Pink => "Pink",
            CueColor::White => "White",
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
pub enum MidiTrigger {
    NoteOn {
        channel: u8,
        note: u8,
    },
    CC {
        channel: u8,
        cc: u8,
        value: Option<u8>,
    },
}

fn default_custom_fade_points() -> Vec<[f32; 2]> {
    vec![[0.0, 0.0], [1.0, 1.0]]
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Cue {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub number: String,
    pub name: String,
    pub color: CueColor,
    pub notes: String,
    pub armed: bool,
    pub cue_type: CueType,
    pub pre_wait_secs: f64,
    pub post_wait_secs: f64,
    pub continue_mode: ContinueMode,
    #[serde(skip)]
    pub state: CueState,
    #[serde(skip)]
    pub position_secs: f64,
    pub file_path: PathBuf,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub audio_channels: usize,
    pub volume: f32,
    pub fade_in_secs: f64,
    pub fade_out_secs: f64,
    pub fade_curve: FadeCurve,
    #[serde(default = "default_custom_fade_points")]
    pub custom_fade_points: Vec<[f32; 2]>,
    pub start_secs: f64,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub end_secs: Option<f64>,
    pub loop_region: LoopRegion,
    pub io: IoRouting,
    pub midi_trigger: Option<MidiTrigger>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub hotkey: Option<String>,
    #[serde(skip)]
    pub waveform: Vec<f32>,
    #[serde(skip)]
    pub duration_secs: f64,
}

impl Cue {
    pub fn new_audio(file_path: PathBuf) -> Self {
        let name = file_path
            .file_stem()
            .and_then(|s| s.to_str())
            .unwrap_or("Untitled")
            .to_string();
        Self {
            id: Uuid::new_v4(),
            number: String::new(),
            name,
            color: CueColor::None,
            notes: String::new(),
            armed: true,
            cue_type: CueType::Audio,
            pre_wait_secs: 0.0,
            post_wait_secs: 0.0,
            continue_mode: ContinueMode::Nothing,
            state: CueState::Stopped,
            position_secs: 0.0,
            file_path,
            audio_channels: 2,
            volume: 1.0,
            fade_in_secs: 0.0,
            fade_out_secs: 0.0,
            fade_curve: FadeCurve::Linear,
            custom_fade_points: default_custom_fade_points(),
            start_secs: 0.0,
            end_secs: None,
            loop_region: LoopRegion::default(),
            io: IoRouting::default(),
            midi_trigger: None,
            hotkey: None,
            waveform: vec![],
            duration_secs: 0.0,
        }
    }

    pub fn new_typed(cue_type: CueType) -> Self {
        let name = cue_type.label().to_string();
        Self {
            id: Uuid::new_v4(),
            number: String::new(),
            name,
            color: CueColor::None,
            notes: String::new(),
            armed: true,
            cue_type,
            pre_wait_secs: 0.0,
            post_wait_secs: 0.0,
            continue_mode: ContinueMode::Nothing,
            state: CueState::Stopped,
            position_secs: 0.0,
            file_path: PathBuf::new(),
            audio_channels: 0,
            volume: 1.0,
            fade_in_secs: 0.0,
            fade_out_secs: 0.0,
            fade_curve: FadeCurve::Linear,
            custom_fade_points: default_custom_fade_points(),
            start_secs: 0.0,
            end_secs: None,
            loop_region: LoopRegion::default(),
            io: IoRouting::default(),
            midi_trigger: None,
            hotkey: None,
            waveform: vec![],
            duration_secs: 0.0,
        }
    }

    pub fn is_active(&self) -> bool {
        self.state.is_active()
    }

    pub fn effective_end_secs(&self) -> f64 {
        self.end_secs
            .unwrap_or(self.duration_secs)
            .min(self.duration_secs)
            .max(self.start_secs)
    }

    pub fn effective_duration(&self) -> f64 {
        match &self.cue_type {
            CueType::Audio => (self.effective_end_secs() - self.start_secs).max(0.0),
            CueType::Fade { duration_secs, .. } => *duration_secs,
            _ => self.duration_secs,
        }
    }

    pub fn progress(&self) -> f32 {
        let eff_end = self.effective_end_secs();
        let span = (eff_end - self.start_secs).max(0.001);
        if eff_end <= 0.0 {
            0.0
        } else {
            ((self.position_secs - self.start_secs) / span).clamp(0.0, 1.0) as f32
        }
    }

    pub fn remaining_secs(&self) -> f64 {
        (self.effective_end_secs() - self.position_secs).max(0.0)
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod fade_status_tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn fade_status_uses_runtime_elapsed_time() {
        let mut cue = Cue::new_typed(CueType::Fade {
            target_id: None,
            fade_in: true,
            duration_secs: 4.0,
            stop_on_finish: false,
            target_volume: None,
            fade_curve: FadeCurve::Linear,
            custom_fade_points: default_custom_fade_points(),
        });
        cue.state = CueState::FadingIn;
        cue.duration_secs = 4.0;
        cue.position_secs = 1.5;

        assert!((cue.progress() - 0.375).abs() < 0.001);
        assert!((cue.remaining_secs() - 2.5).abs() < 0.001);
    }
}


// === FILE: D:\winlab\cuelabs\src\cue\cue_list.rs ===

// src/cue/cue_list.rs вЂ” СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє РєСЊСЋ

use crate::cue::{Cue, CueState, CueType};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, Default)]
pub struct CueList {
    pub name: String,
    pub cues: Vec<Cue>,
}

impl CueList {
    pub fn new(name: &str) -> Self {
        Self {
            name: name.to_string(),
            cues: vec![],
        }
    }

    pub fn add(&mut self, mut cue: Cue) {
        if cue.number.trim().is_empty() {
            let next_number = self
                .cues
                .iter()
                .filter_map(|existing| existing.number.trim().parse::<u64>().ok())
                .max()
                .unwrap_or(0)
                .saturating_add(1);
            cue.number = next_number.to_string();
        }
        self.cues.push(cue);
    }

    pub fn remove(&mut self, id: Uuid) {
        // Cue numbers are stable identifiers used by operators and target labels.
        // Removing a cue must not silently renumber the remaining project.
        self.cues.retain(|c| c.id != id);
    }

    pub fn find(&self, id: Uuid) -> Option<&Cue> {
        self.cues.iter().find(|c| c.id == id)
    }

    pub fn find_mut(&mut self, id: Uuid) -> Option<&mut Cue> {
        self.cues.iter_mut().find(|c| c.id == id)
    }

    pub fn renumber(&mut self) {
        for (i, cue) in self.cues.iter_mut().enumerate() {
            cue.number = format!("{}", i + 1);
        }
    }

    pub fn active_count(&self) -> usize {
        self.cues.iter().filter(|c| c.is_active()).count()
    }

    pub fn pause_all(&mut self) {
        for c in &mut self.cues {
            if matches!(
                c.state,
                CueState::Playing | CueState::FadingIn | CueState::FadingOut
            ) {
                c.state = CueState::Paused;
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn resume_all(&mut self) {
        for c in &mut self.cues {
            if c.state == CueState::Paused {
                c.state = CueState::Playing;
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn stop_all(&mut self) {
        for c in &mut self.cues {
            c.state = CueState::Stopped;
            c.position_secs = 0.0;
        }
    }

    pub fn move_up(&mut self, id: Uuid) {
        if let Some(i) = self.cues.iter().position(|c| c.id == id) {
            if i > 0 {
                self.cues.swap(i, i - 1);
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn move_down(&mut self, id: Uuid) {
        let len = self.cues.len();
        if let Some(i) = self.cues.iter().position(|c| c.id == id) {
            if i + 1 < len {
                self.cues.swap(i, i + 1);
            }
        }
    }

    /// Р’СЃРµ Р°СѓРґРёРѕ-РєСЊСЋ РІ РїСЂРѕРµРєС‚Рµ (РґР»СЏ РІС‹Р±РѕСЂР° target РІ Play / Pause / Stop / Fade)
    pub fn audio_targets(&self) -> Vec<(Uuid, String, std::path::PathBuf)> {
        self.cues
            .iter()
            .filter(|c| {
                matches!(&c.cue_type, CueType::Audio) || !c.file_path.as_os_str().is_empty()
            })
            .map(|c| {
                let fname = c
                    .file_path
                    .file_name()
                    .and_then(|n| n.to_str())
                    .unwrap_or("no file");
                (
                    c.id,
                    format!("#{} вЂ” {} ({})", c.number, c.name, fname),
                    c.file_path.clone(),
                )
            })
            .collect()
    }
}

// === FILE: D:\winlab\cuelabs\src\export\score_export.rs ===

// src/export/score_export.rs вЂ” РіРµРЅРµСЂР°С†РёСЏ С‚РµРєСЃС‚Р° РїР°СЂС‚РёС‚СѓСЂС‹ РїРѕ С€Р°Р±Р»РѕРЅСѓ

use crate::cue::CueList;
use anyhow::Result;
use std::path::Path;

#[allow(dead_code)]
pub const DEFAULT_TEMPLATE: &str = "{number}\t{name}\t{duration}\t{notes}";

/// РЁР°Р±Р»РѕРЅРЅС‹Рµ РїРµСЂРµРјРµРЅРЅС‹Рµ:
/// {number}, {name}, {file}, {duration}, {notes}, {fade_in}, {fade_out}
#[allow(dead_code)]
pub fn export_score(cue_list: &CueList, template: &str, output_path: &Path) -> Result<()> {
    let mut lines = Vec::new();

    for cue in &cue_list.cues {
        let duration = format_duration(cue.effective_duration());
        let line = template
            .replace("{number}", &cue.number)
            .replace("{name}", &cue.name)
            .replace(
                "{file}",
                &cue.file_path
                    .file_name()
                    .and_then(|n| n.to_str())
                    .unwrap_or(""),
            )
            .replace("{duration}", &duration)
            .replace("{notes}", &cue.notes)
            .replace("{fade_in}", &format!("{:.1}", cue.fade_in_secs))
            .replace("{fade_out}", &format!("{:.1}", cue.fade_out_secs));
        lines.push(line);
    }

    let content = lines.join("\n");
    std::fs::write(output_path, content)?;
    Ok(())
}

#[allow(dead_code)]
fn format_duration(secs: f64) -> String {
    let m = (secs / 60.0) as u32;
    let s = secs as u32 % 60;
    let ms = ((secs.fract()) * 1000.0) as u32;
    format!("{m:02}:{s:02}.{ms:03}")
}
